  leaf (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) is the \isa{leaf} hypothesis; the
  union- and sub-relation back-loops are thin instantiations.
  Throughout, \isa{b} abbreviates \isa{block{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}width\ {\isasymGamma}} (the per-symbol cell width).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The write back-walk loop, proper tape.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} stage at bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} with the head at
  the block end \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b} (where the read phase left it) and
  \isa{buf\ tk} a proper symbol, the head walks \isa{L} across the
  \isa{b}-cell block back to \isa{base\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ p}, reaching
  bit-counter \isa{b}.  Tape unchanged (the back-walk only moves);
  this is the simpler of the two write loops, the analogue of
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} with a constant-tape invariant.  Each
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} needs its current cell \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}};
  the visited cells are \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}\ {\isasymdots}\ base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b} (all proper
  positions), supplied by \isa{notLE}.  Chain length \isa{b}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
\isakeywordONE{lemma}\isamarkupfalse%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The write forward bit-write loop, proper tape.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} stage at bit-counter \isa{b} with the head back
  at \isa{base} (where \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}back{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} left it), the head
  walks \isa{R} across the block writing each cell, reaching
  bit-counter \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} with the head at \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  Unlike the read loops this loop \emph{mutates} the
  tape: its invariant carries a partially-overwritten tape (cells
  \isa{base\ {\isasymdots}\ base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ j\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} already hold the new
  \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit} image, the rest hold old content), and each
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}'s \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} guard reads the
  \emph{not-yet-written} current cell \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ j}, which
  still holds old content (supplied \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} by
  \isa{notLE}).  Writes the first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} cells; the last is
  the boundary step's job.  Chain length \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%

  bit-writing leaf (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) is the \isa{leaf}
  hypothesis; the union- and sub-relation forward-loops are thin
  instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape write prefix: the part of a
  proper-cell single-tape write shared by the non-last
  (\isa{boundary}) and last (\isa{finish}) variants.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} stage at bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} with the head at
  the block end \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b} and \isa{buf\ tk} a proper symbol,
  it fires the back-walk loop (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}back{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop}, head to
  \isa{base}) and the forward bit-write loop
  (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}fwd{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop}, writing the first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}
  cells), reaching bit-counter \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} with the head at
  \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  The remaining last-cell step (boundary or
  finish) is added by the consumer.  The two segment costs compose by
  \isa{relpow{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}add} on \isa{relcompI} (chain length \isa{b\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}).  The \isa{notLE} hypothesis (every block cell and the
  next-block lead cell \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}}) feeds both loops.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write proper-prefix scaffold.  The two
  loop helpers (back, forward) are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}back} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}fwd} hypotheses; the union- and sub-relation prefixes
  are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Extend a written block prefix by its last cell.  Both
  proper-write finishers reach a tape whose first \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} block
  cells already hold the new \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit} image; the boundary /
  finish step writes the last cell \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  This
  \isa{fun{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}upd} closes the gap: updating the \isa{b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}-prefix
  function at \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} with \isa{f\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} yields
  the full \isa{b}-cell write.  Pure list/function arithmetic, shared
  by \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} and
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}.%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape write, non-last tape: the prefix
  followed by the bit-boundary step (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}),
  writing the last block cell \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}b\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} and landing at the
  next tape's \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk}, counter
  \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}).  After the \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b}-step walk the \isa{b}-cell
  block at \isa{base} spells out \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} (the
  encoded new symbol), every other cell unchanged, and the head returns
  to the block end \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b}.  Chain length \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write proper-step scaffold.  The prefix
  walk and the closing bit-boundary step are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix}
  and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary} hypotheses; union and sub versions are
  thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The proper-cell single-tape write, last tape: as
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} but \isa{tk} is the last tape, so after
  the last-cell write (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) the phase
  transitions to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} with the current-tape field reset
  to \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}} (all tapes written).  Same \isa{b}-cell block
  write and head return to \isa{base\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b}.  Chain length \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write proper-finish-step scaffold.  As
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}gen} but the closing leaf is the
  last-tape finish (transition to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance}); the prefix
  and finish helpers are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish} hypotheses.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The unified single-tape write, non-last tape: the LE/proper
  dispatch, the AR analogue of \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} stage at bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, dispatch on
  \isa{posk\ tk\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtLE} (which the caller pins to \isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}},
  \isa{M}'s head on the marker): the LE arm
  (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}, \isa{{\isadigit{1}}} step) leaves the tape and head
  untouched and hands off; the proper arm (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step},
  \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b} steps) overwrites the \isa{b}-cell block at \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ p} with \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}.  In \emph{both}
  arms every head is unchanged (the LE arm is all-\isa{N}; the proper
  arm back-walks then returns to the block end), so the conclusion is
  \isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ c{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ c{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}} uniformly.  The proper-arm
  \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} facts come from the input correspondence
  \isa{tcorr} (block-\isa{p} cells and the block-\isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}p{\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}
  lead cell, all \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr} cells via
  \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}nth{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}not{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}LE{\isadigit{4}}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write single-tape step scaffold (non-last).
  The two cases (\isa{p\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}} le-step, \isa{p\ {\isasymnoteq}\ {\isadigit{0}}} proper-step)
  are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le} and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper} hypotheses; union and
  sub versions are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The unified single-tape write, last tape: as
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} but \isa{tk} is the last tape, so both
  arms transition to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} (current-tape field reset to
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}, all tapes written): the LE arm via
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}, the proper arm via
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}.  Same tape edit and head
  invariance.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write single-tape finish-step scaffold
  (last tape).  As \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}gen} but both cases use
  the last-tape finish leaves (transition to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance});
  the le-finish and proper-finish helpers are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}proper} hypotheses.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The write-phase prefix walk: from the write boundary
  (current-tape field \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}, bit-counter
  \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, the head positions the read phase left), iterate the
  unified non-last per-tape write \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} over the
  first \isa{j} tapes (\isa{j\ {\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}, all non-last),
  landing back at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} on tape \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ j}.
  Dual to \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}read{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix}: the read kept the tape constant and
  varied \isa{buf}/\isa{posk}; the write keeps
  \isa{buf}/\isa{posk}/positions constant and varies the
  \emph{tape}.  The descriptor splits on \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ j}: visited tapes carry their overwritten block
  (\isa{{\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}out}, the \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit} image for proper tapes, or
  the unchanged tape for \isa{LE} tapes); the rest are unchanged.
  Each per-tape step's \isa{tcorr} / position entry facts hold
  because the current tape is not yet visited
  (\isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape\ c\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ j\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape\ c{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ j{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}).  Variable
  per-tape cost (\isa{{\isadigit{1}}} or \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b}), aggregate bounded by
  \isa{j\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isadigit{2}}b}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic write prefix walk scaffold.  The single
  per-tape helper (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) is the \isa{leaf}
  hypothesis, quantified over the per-tape config, tape index,
  tape-content function, and position; union and sub versions are
  thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The full write phase: the prefix walk over the first
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} tapes followed by the unified last-tape write
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}, landing at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance}
  (current-tape field \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}) with every proper tape's
  \isa{b}-cell block overwritten by \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ k{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} (the
  encoded \isa{M}-write symbol) and every \isa{LE} tape and head
  position unchanged.  Aggregate cost \isa{{\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isadigit{2}}b}.
  The split tape descriptor collapses to the full per-tape overwrite on
  the last tape (every other tape has index \isa{{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
